NSE-A WHOLE-SPACE EHRENFEST — USABLE LEAN SOURCE INDEX
Version 0.2 — 21 November 2026
Companion to NSE_A_EHRENFEST_INTERMEDIATE_PLAN_B_TIER_v1_0.txt
Scope: declaration indexing and source/evidence review for steps I01–I16.
No new proof, native endpoint, compiler run, kernel audit, repository write, or CI trigger.
Local Lean/Lake executions: none.

0. WHAT THIS INDEX CONTAINS
  56 curated source cards; 175 declaration entries; 175 distinct names.
  49 distinct pinned repository/revision/source-file locations.
  92 selected names have records in the accepted main integration map.
  16 additional selected names have inspected cross-branch named-audit records.
  Those counts include definitions, instances and structures as well as theorems.
  Some cards deliberately retain private or domain-incompatible source as proof
  patterns or scope guards. The total is not a count of drop-in R3 theorems.
  Source headers and line spans are retained; every card states the adapter still needed.
  This is a cumulative curated index of relevant parts, not an exhaustive repository catalog.

0.1. ADDITIONS IN VERSION 0.2
  Preserved all 38 v0.1 cards and all 131 original names.
  Added 18 source cards and 44 distinct declaration names.
  AN: actual Lp products, Young absorption, and inner/norm-square time calculus.
  EP: exact periodic error and transport patterns with domain, sign and norm guards.
  EX: lower spectral realization/interpolation and external H1 convection references.
  UC: compact-reference-image uniform convergence from a common contraction bound.
  Section 4.1 records a quantitative H2-to-H1 cutoff-tail adapter target. It is
  a construction note, not an existing named Lean theorem or an accepted endpoint.

1. HOW TO READ THE EVIDENCE AND SIGNATURES
  ACCEPTED AT EXISTING SCOPE means retained external evidence was inspected and
  source identity was matched. It does not accept a new R3 adapter or conclusion.
  PINNED SOURCE means the actual declaration was read at the dependency/source pin;
  no separate named acceptance is inferred solely from its presence.
  PROOF PATTERN means the reasoning can guide new work, while its current type
  retains a different domain, mask, family, or other incompatible premise.
  PRIVATE means the displayed name is a source-local template, not a public import.
  DEFINITION/STRUCTURE means an object or predicate was declared; no inhabitant
  or all-data existence theorem follows merely from acceptance of that declaration.

  Each signature is a source statement/header, with a short definitional equation
  where useful. It is not an independently compiled cutout. Ambient section
  variables, typeclasses, namespace and open-scope context must be preserved;
  see REQUIRED INPUTS and LIMITATIONS. Printed-type hashes identify retained
  reports; they are not substitutes for the full independent consumer needed
  when a new endpoint is later formalized.

  A named integration record is appended per declaration when an exact name
  matches the retained accepted map. Cross-branch named records retain their
  own tested source. Card-level source-only/mixed classifications are preserved.
  Later accepted receipts supersede old 'prepared' source comments only for
  the matched source and named scope. No new A-tier signal is issued by this index.

The step mapping indicates support, not closure. In particular I04, I06,
I10–I11 and I14 need actual new PDE constructions even when many lower
lemmas have already been accepted.

4. FIRST REUSE SET
  I01: LC01–LC02 and PC01–PC02; preserve the LC03 periodic-domain guard.
  I02: PC03–PC09, PC12–PC13, MV02–MV03 and MV12.
       Construct the ball symbol; prove the new projection/commutation/tail lemmas.
  I03: PC02, PC10–PC12, MV01–MV02; construct bandlimited derivative witnesses
       and actual Bernstein representatives. Closed-carrier machinery alone
       does not prove those bounds.
  I04–I05: PC06–PC11, AN01–AN03 and MV05–MV07; first supply quantitative
       nonlinear bounds on norm balls, then invoke the dimension-free ODE theorem.
  I06–I07: SC07, MV09 and the physical graph identities; derive convection
       cancellation and actual time differentiation using AN04–AN05. External
       convection cards are references with a separate type/toolchain adapter.
       MV10 is not a drop-in solver.
  I09: AN01–AN03 and EX cards support nonsharp product/interpolation admission;
       EX05 -> EX02 -> EX01 supplies the lower interpolation chain for an
       actual PhysicalH2 value. The native norm rewrite and quantitative Lp
       admission are still distinct implementation steps.
  I10: PC11–PC12 give the exact Fourier weights. Section 4.1 gives the direct
       H2 tail target; UC01–UC03 provide a compact-uniform alternative. EP cards
       retain the periodic residual sign and stronger reference norm premises.
  I11–I13: I09 product admission plus SC01 and SC02; retain the full H1 error
       and the strict positivity of the small forcing sequence.
       Use the EP algebra with AN estimates, rebuilding the full-space bounds.
  I14: LC01 supplies only the PDE predicate. No indexed theorem supplies the
       full literal finite-energy closed-slab Sobolev admission.

4.1. CONSTRUCTION NOTE: QUANTITATIVE CUTOFF TAIL
  Status: a proposed new adapter justified by the indexed physical Fourier
  identities. No new Lean declaration or compiler acceptance is claimed.
  Sources: PC11 physicalH2_norm_sq_eq_fourier and PC12
  physicalH1_norm_sq_eq_fourier, with the actual ball-mask construction in I02.
  Their Fourier convention gives q = 4*pi^2*||xi||^2, H1 weight 1+q, and
  H2 weight 1+q+q^2. For N>0 and ||xi||>N, put a=4*pi^2*N^2>0. Then
    a*(1+q) <= q*(1+q) <= 1+q+q^2,
  hence 1+q <= (1+q+q^2)/a on the Fourier tail.
  Once J_N has the actual Fourier indicator formula and the physical H1
  derivative adapter, the intended conclusion for U : PhysicalH2 is
    ||(I-J_N)U||_H1^2 <= ||U||_H2^2 / (4*pi^2*N^2),
    ||(I-J_N)U||_H1 <= ||U||_H2 / (2*pi*N).
  Thus a supplied bound ||U(t)||_H2 <= M on [0,T], with N=n+1, gives
    delta_n = sup_{t in [0,T]} ||(I-J_n)U(t)||_H1
            <= M / (2*pi*(n+1)) -> 0.
  This can supply I10's uniform consistency estimate without an additional
  compact-image proof for this H2 reference. UC01–UC03 remain useful when
  only H1 continuity, contraction and pointwise approximation are supplied.
  This norm bound does not construct J_N, prove its derivative compatibility,
  admit a literal NSE-A reference into H2, or estimate its nonlinear residual.

LC01 — Literal whole-space classical PDE contract
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/PhysicalRoot.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.PhysicalRoot
Git blob: cfe780efd559de7dc699829b95834077a72f1265
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/PhysicalRoot.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.PhysicalRoot
Plan steps: I01, I14
Reuse classification: DEFINITION_REUSE_WITH_ADAPTER
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED_NAMED_TYPE; STRUCTURE_ONLY_NO_EXISTENCE

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Defines genuine unforced Euclidean NSE and joint smoothness on t>=delta, with no spatial periodicity. This is a predicate, not a proof of a solution or of Sobolev admission.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Uses actual SingularClosure.Velocity/Pressure and Euclidean operators; the associated tailSpacetime is {z : Real x Space | delta <= z.1}. A witness must supply every field, including p(t,0)=0.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Use delta=0 for the PDE/smoothness part of literal NSE-A; add the actual Schwartz initial equality and the global bounded-L2-energy requirement. Prove equivalence with the unrestricted additive-pressure-gauge category. For I14 derive physical Sobolev and projected-equation admission, rather than adding them as contract fields.

LIMITATIONS
  Point normalization p(t,0)=0 is a smooth time gauge, not the Fourier/Riesz pressure normalization and not a pressure-L2 theorem. No global all-data QFA or NSE-A existence is provided.

DECLARATIONS

  LC01.01 — MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.PhysicalRoot.ClassicalTail
  Kind: structure
  Source statement lines: 42–51
  Source signature:
    structure ClassicalTail (ν δ : ℝ)
        (v : SingularClosure.Velocity) (p : SingularClosure.Pressure) : Prop where
      velocity_smooth : ContDiffOn ℝ ∞ (fun z : ℝ × Space => v z.1 z.2) (tailSpacetime δ)
      pressure_smooth : ContDiffOn ℝ ∞ (fun z : ℝ × Space => p z.1 z.2) (tailSpacetime δ)
      divergence_free : ∀ t : ℝ, δ ≤ t → ∀ x : Space,
        SingularClosure.divergence (v t) x = 0
      equation : ∀ t : ℝ, δ ≤ t → ∀ x : Space,
        SingularClosure.timeDerivative v t x + SingularClosure.convection (v t) x +
            SingularClosure.gradient (p t) x = ν • SingularClosure.laplacian (v t) x
      pressure_normalized : ∀ t : ℝ, δ ≤ t → p t 0 = 0
  Retained main-integration named record:
    Endpoint: QFAZeroDatum
    Printed type SHA256: 1473cfd58168ecf30084a3c3de738065d51f3e8245b172d151685f4b8e5e7492
    Axioms: Classical.choice, Quot.sound, propext
    Accepted baseline(s): ac_000c1bd9
    Map: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/audits/integration-5a44e985-accepted/endpoint-comparison-map.json

LC02 — Domain-neutral Euclidean field and differential operators
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/_vendor/SingularClosure/Contracts.lean
Import module: SingularClosure.Contracts
Git blob: 7d64a61a47bedb2bd7a2801641c5959178837f04
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/_vendor/SingularClosure/Contracts.lean
Namespace: SingularClosure
Plan steps: I01, I04, I06, I07, I14
Reuse classification: DEFINITION_REUSE
Evidence status:
  EXACT_SOURCE_INSPECTED; IMPORTED_BY_ACCEPTED_CLASSICALTAIL; NO_SEPARATE_NAMED_AUDITS_LOCATED

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Supplies actual Euclidean carriers and classical differential operators. These particular definitions contain no periodicity predicate.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Classical derivatives are total Lean operations; callers must prove the required differentiability before using analytical identities. Vec=EuclideanSpace Real (Fin3).

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Identify classical field representatives with actual SpatialL2/PhysicalH1/PhysicalH2 classes and their weak derivatives. For a bilinear B(u,v), construct the mixed convection map explicitly: the existing convection definition is the diagonal fderiv a x (a x).

LIMITATIONS
  timeDerivative uses derivWithin on Ici0; at the initial endpoint it is the right derivative, and at positive times it matches the ordinary derivative only after the applicable neighborhood/differentiability proof. Other predicates in this same module are periodic; importing its operator definitions does not authorize reusing those predicates for R3.

LC03 — Periodic contract boundary: do not substitute these for NSE-A
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/_vendor/SingularClosure/Contracts.lean
Import module: SingularClosure.Contracts
Git blob: 7d64a61a47bedb2bd7a2801641c5959178837f04
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/_vendor/SingularClosure/Contracts.lean
Namespace: SingularClosure
Plan steps: I01, I14, I15
Reuse classification: EXCLUDED_FROM_R3_TARGET; SEMANTIC_GUARD
Evidence status:
  EXACT_SOURCE_INSPECTED; NO_NEW_AUDIT_CLAIM

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Defines the periodic smooth mean-zero datum and periodic global solution categories used by the accepted torus route.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  SmoothSigmaZero includes SpatialPeriodic and MeanZero. GlobalSmoothNSE requires periodic velocity and pressure and both mean normalizations.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Retain these for the periodic program. Define the new whole-space target using the genuine Euclidean PDE plus Schwartz data and full-space finite energy.

LIMITATIONS
  The coordinate carrier is Euclidean, but the predicate is periodic. Dropping periodic fields is a new theorem/contract construction, not a reinterpretation of the accepted periodic theorem.

DECLARATIONS

  Kind: structure

  Source signature:

      smooth : ContDiff ℝ ∞ a
      periodic : SpatialPeriodic a
      divergence_free : ∀ x : Vec, divergence a x = 0
      mean_zero : MeanZero a
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

  LC03.02 — SingularClosure.GlobalSmoothNSE
  Kind: structure
  Source statement lines: 96–110
  Source signature:
    structure GlobalSmoothNSE (ν : ℝ) (a : Field)
        (u : Velocity) (p : Pressure) : Prop where
      velocity_smooth :
        ContDiffOn ℝ ∞ (fun z : ℝ × Vec => u z.1 z.2) forwardSpacetime
      pressure_smooth :
        ContDiffOn ℝ ∞ (fun z : ℝ × Vec => p z.1 z.2) forwardSpacetime
      velocity_periodic : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → SpatialPeriodic (u t)
      pressure_periodic : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → SpatialPeriodic (p t)
      divergence_free : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → ∀ x : Vec, divergence (u t) x = 0
      equation : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → ∀ x : Vec,
        timeDerivative u t x + convection (u t) x + gradient (p t) x =
          ν • laplacian (u t) x
      initial : u 0 = a
      velocity_mean_zero : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → MeanZero (u t)
      pressure_mean_zero : ∀ t : ℝ, 0 ≤ t → PressureMeanZero (p t)
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

EVIDENCE / SOURCE POINTERS
  https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/Definitions.lean
    Existing periodic SmoothDatum and NSE_B depend on these categories; therefore the accepted periodic root cannot be substituted for the new R3 root.

==============================================================================

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Builds physical H1 as a closed graph of the native compact-test derivatives, proves its complete-space instances and exact inhomogeneous graph norm, and constructs physical H1 representatives of Schwartz fields. The solenoidal Schwartz constructor retains the explicit native Solenoidal proof.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT

LIMITATIONS
  Completeness is proved for the graph, not for a newly defined bandlimited phase space. The Schwartz-to-sigma constructor does not by itself prove the pointwise-divergence-to-Solenoidal adapter. No H1-to-L6 inequality, nonlinear flow, or temporal differentiation is established here.

PC03 — Actual real Fourier range via Hermitian symmetry
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/RealFourier.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.RealFourier
Git blob: 8d916d5d2c957c17e0c3ba94aad921f94dd3cb9f
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/RealFourier.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.RealFourier
Plan steps: I02, I03
Reuse classification: Constructive Fourier reality/range theorem
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs the conjugate-reflection operator on actual complex-vector L2 and characterizes exactly which frequency classes are Fourier transforms of real spatial L2 fields. The Fourier transform of any actual real field is proved Hermitian.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  An actual complex L2 frequency field f. To recover a real spatial field, prove hermitianL2CLM f = f; this is a checkable Fourier symmetry, not an assumed real inverse-transform witness.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Use the range theorem with bounded derivative symbols restricted to the frequency ball to construct real L2 derivatives. Keep the existing complexification and L2 Fourier isometry conventions.

LIMITATIONS
  This proves L2 reality and range, not continuous or smooth pointwise representatives, Bernstein bounds, compact-set convergence, or a bandlimited Hm constructor. The underlying Plancherel isometry is imported from the pinned Mathlib/vendor layer.

Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H1Fourier.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H1Fourier
Plan steps: I02, I03, I07, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Theorem on actual supplied weak derivatives
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Derives distributional and almost-everywhere Fourier derivative identities from literal real compact-test derivative hypotheses. The real physical-gradient theorem uses complexification and the exact 2*pi*i directional symbol.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Actual L2 fields u and du, or v and G, satisfying the corresponding native weak-derivative predicate. The first two indexed declarations use ComplexL2 with real scalar tests; the physical-gradient declaration uses SpatialL2.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Instantiate with the derivatives of PhysicalH1/PhysicalH2 fields and with constructed cutoff derivatives. Apply Fourier injectivity to transport commutation and spectral identities back to the physical graph.

LIMITATIONS
  The derivative fields are inputs. No arbitrary weighted-Sobolev derivative existence, H2 assumption, PDE, or time regularity is supplied by this forward bridge.

DECLARATIONS

PC05 — Reverse Fourier derivative bridge with explicit witnesses
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H1FourierReverse.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H1FourierReverse
Git blob: 1b290db1474b4c445e1bbdbc3b4fa56530c6188d
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H1FourierReverse.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H1Fourier
Plan steps: I02, I03, I10
Reuse classification: Supplied-witness conditional input; genuine reverse theorem
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Identifies supplied real L2 derivative fields from their literal almost-everywhere Fourier multiplier relations, and proves the exact two-way equivalence for a supplied three-component physical gradient.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  u and du must already be actual RealL2 fields, or u and all G j must be SpatialL2. Supply the displayed almost-everywhere equality with (2*pi*i)*inner(xi,e).

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  First construct real inverse-Fourier L2 fields for the bounded-on-the-ball derivative symbols, using PC03 and L2 multiplier estimates; then invoke these reverse theorems to prove native weak derivatives and create PhysicalH1/PhysicalH2 values.

LIMITATIONS
  The equivalences do not construct derivative witnesses merely from a weighted integral bound. Treating the supplied G as an already-created cutoff derivative would skip a real I03 obligation. Both H1Fourier files intentionally use the same namespace.

DECLARATIONS

PC06 — Constructed bounded real-even scalar multipliers
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/RealEvenMultipliers.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.RealEvenMultipliers
Git blob: 8882c6f762d2bd8b0edbe5b95c0e1a524fb7eb7a
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/RealEvenMultipliers.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.RealEvenMultipliers
Plan steps: I02, I03, I04, I10
Reuse classification: Actual generic construction; specialize to a ball-mask symbol
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs bounded real-linear Fourier operators from measurable, even, bounded real symbols; proves their exact Fourier action, norm bound, uniqueness by Fourier data, and commutation with native weak gradients. Symbol smoothness is not needed.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  A RealEvenSymbol with literal value, Measurable, evenness, a finite NNReal bound and a pointwise norm bound. For weak-gradient transport, provide SpatialWeakGradient v G.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Define the sharp ball indicator m_n and verify its elementary RealEvenSymbol fields. Derive idempotence, self-adjointness, cutoff composition and Leray commutation from its Fourier formula. Lift to H1 and H2 using derivative commutation twice and graph constructors; prove strong and compact-trajectory approximation.

LIMITATIONS
  The generic record permits many symbols; it does not assert that every such operator is a projection. The sharp ball instance, self-adjoint/projection laws, H2 lift, strong approximation, Bernstein estimates and regularized dynamics are not among these accepted declarations.

DECLARATIONS

PC07 — Actual real L2 Leray projection and scalar commutation
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LerayMultiplier.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LerayMultiplier
Git blob: 30cf74d057862eaad92f1029ce7cde3e90d0a877
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LerayMultiplier.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LerayMultiplier
Plan steps: I02, I03, I04, I06, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Constructed real L2 orthogonal projection; generic scalar-symbol commutation
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Provides the actual bounded real-linear Leray operator, L2 contraction, idempotence and self-adjoint inner-product identity. The frequency multiplier theorem transports a supplied scalar Fourier relation through the matrix Leray projection.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Arbitrary actual SpatialL2 fields for the real projection identities. frequencyLeray_multiplier_ae requires actual complex L2 f and g plus a supplied almost-everywhere scalar-multiplier relation; the scalar m itself need not be globally bounded in that theorem.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Combine the scalar-commutation theorem with the ball-mask Fourier action to obtain J_n P = P J_n. Use self-adjointness and fixed-point results in PC08 to remove P inside physical energy pairings. Construct the nonlinear convection product and its L2 membership separately.

LIMITATIONS
  No nonlinear RHS, convection cancellation, pressure construction, or time evolution follows from the operator identities alone. The supplied scalar relation is not an existence theorem for g.

PC08 — Exact all-L2 solenoidal range of Leray
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LeraySolenoidal.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LeraySolenoidal
Git blob: 423889f6957b5811464614ca3923adaf76ea53a7
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LeraySolenoidal.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LeraySolenoidal
Plan steps: I01, I03, I04, I06, I10
Reuse classification: Unconditional native all-L2 range and fixed-point theorems
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Proves that the original compact-test Solenoidal predicate is equivalent to Fourier transversality for every actual SpatialL2 field. Consequently the real Leray fixed points and its actual range are exactly the native solenoidal fields.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Only an actual SpatialL2 field. No weak-gradient/H1 membership or supplied spectral equivalence is required by these three statements.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Express the bandlimited solenoidal space using the actual Leray and cutoff fixed-point equations. Prove its closedness through continuous maps and derive invariance of the regularized RHS.

LIMITATIONS
  These theorems establish the L2 range, not a smooth pressure, weak-strong uniqueness, existence of a Navier-Stokes trajectory, or a statement about arbitrary pointwise fields before their L2 admission.

PC09 — Native H1 Leray transport and range
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LerayH1.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LerayH1
Git blob: 9ab3051fa6e5cf7b86e5e343321960eed00c45ed
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/LerayH1.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.LerayH1
Plan steps: I02, I04, I07, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Constructed H1 lift with proved weak-gradient transport
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Projects both the actual velocity and each actual weak derivative, constructs the bounded physical-H1 Leray map, proves H1 contraction and identifies its range using the original Solenoidal predicate on the physical velocity.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  A PhysicalH1 input for the H1 operator and range statements. The lower derivative transport theorem takes an actual SpatialWeakGradient witness and proves the projected witness.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Use the H1 contraction and derivative transport in the full inhomogeneous error estimate. Construct any H2 Leray lift by applying the same derivative transport to the Hessian and packaging it; specialize commutation to the sharp cutoff.

LIMITATIONS
  The indexed H1 range result does not itself supply an H2 operator, a nonlinear estimate, H1 time differentiation, or the regularized flow. Six-sector commutation exists elsewhere in the same file but is not a ball-mask theorem.

  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Builds the thirteen-component physical H2 graph with genuine weak gradient and Hessian, proves completeness, and constructs continuous linear maps to H1, to each first derivative as an H1 field, and to the actual L2 Laplacian.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  A PhysicalH2 value for operator identities. physicalH2OfWeakDerivatives requires v, three L2 gradient fields, nine L2 Hessian fields, and proofs of SpatialWeakGradient and SpatialWeakHessian.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  For cutoff fields, construct first and second derivative witnesses before invoking the constructor. Use physicalH2GradientH1 to apply a future physical H1-to-L6 theorem to each gradient component. Use physicalH2ToH1 and the actual Laplacian in time differentiation and error equations.

LIMITATIONS
  The constructor does not infer H2 from pointwise smoothness or finite energy and does not construct arbitrary weighted Fourier witnesses. Completeness and derivative lifts do not prove Bernstein, Sobolev/Agmon, nonlinear products, or temporal regularity.

PC11 — Exact physical Hessian, Laplacian and H2 Fourier identities
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H2Spectral.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H2Spectral
Git blob: 08e98f172b13beced8b0b6009d3da6acf3a8519e
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H2Spectral.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H2Spectral
Plan steps: I02, I03, I07, I09, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Proved physical spectral identities
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Proves the actual L2 Laplacian symbol with the 2*pi normalization, equality of the ordered-pair Hessian norm square and Laplacian norm square, and the full inhomogeneous H2 norm in physical and Fourier forms.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Only an actual PhysicalH2 field. The Fourier identities and norm equalities are conclusions, not supplied spectral premises.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Translate the physical graph norms into the plan's derivative/Laplacian quantities, retaining all ordered Hessian entries and the low-frequency L2 term. Use the spectral formulas to prove cutoff contraction and tail bounds once the H2 cutoff field is constructed.

LIMITATIONS
  These are exact identities, not H2 reconstruction from a fourth-order Fourier weight, H2-to-Linfinity interpolation, product bounds, or NSE enstrophy evolution. No dimension factor or Poincare replacement may be inserted.

PC12 — Physical H1 Fourier norm and enstrophy integrability
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H1Spectral.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H1Spectral
Git blob: d993e2b033c68dd014e54dfcb507ef22ba70f833
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/H1Spectral.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.H1Spectral
Plan steps: I02, I03, I07, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Proved H1-only physical Fourier identities
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact source bytes and all indexed named records match retained external acceptance and the accepted main/AC integration. Source-era pending headers are superseded only for this verified scope. This documentary index creates no new I01-I16 A-tier endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Proves genuine weighted Fourier integrability for a physical H1 field, the exact 4*pi^2 gradient-energy normalization, and the full inhomogeneous H1 Fourier norm used by the error quantity.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Only a PhysicalH1 value; no H2, PDE, finite temporal budget or smooth-reference assumption appears in these signatures.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Apply to the difference of two physical H1 fields and use the ball-mask Fourier formula to derive contraction and uniform tail estimates. Convert physicalH1Enstrophy to the plan's half-enstrophy by the explicit factor 1/2.

LIMITATIONS
  physicalH1Enstrophy is the full gradient norm square, whereas the new e_n is half of that. Weighted integrability does not by itself create a new physical gradient from arbitrary frequency data, and no time derivative or eventual bound is proved here.

==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The kernel of a continuous semilinear map is closed for a T1 codomain; its equalizer with another such map is closed for a T2 codomain. Kernels and equalizers in a complete uniform module inherit CompleteSpace.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Ambient source: semirings R₁,R₂; ring hom σ₁₂:R₁→+*R₂; topological additive commutative monoids M₁,M₂ with Module R₁ M₁ and Module R₂ M₂.
  - Completeness instances explicitly assume UniformSpace and CompleteSpace on the domain. Kernel requires T1Space on the codomain; equalizer requires T2Space.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Represent E_n as ker(Id−J_n), or eqLocus Id J_n, inside the already complete actual L2/H1 carrier; intersect with the solenoidal closed subspace and supply inherited instances.

LIMITATIONS
  - These declarations supply topology/completeness only; they do not construct J_n, bandlimited representatives, solenoidality, or finite-dimensionality.
  - The bandlimited whole-space state space remains infinite-dimensional.

==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4

MV05 — Explicit Picard-Lindelof contract and autonomous constructor
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Analysis/ODE/PicardLindelof.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Analysis.ODE.PicardLindelof
Git blob: a692cd5696e93c9bc84c79499323faa0313baf4a
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Analysis/ODE/PicardLindelof.lean
Namespace: _root_ (IsPicardLindelof); IsPicardLindelof
Plan steps: I04, I05, I06
Reuse classification: DIRECT_BANACH_ODE_HYPOTHESIS_ASSEMBLY
Evidence status:
  PINNED_MATHLIB_SOURCE_INSPECTED_NO_NEW_NAMED_AUDIT. Exact source at the project's dependency pin; this card does not assert a newly accepted consumer or endpoint.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Records Lipschitz and norm bounds on a closed spatial ball, time continuity, and a quantitative interval-size condition. The autonomous constructor discharges time continuity from an actual time-independent vector field.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - The structure and of_time_independent need E:Type* with NormedAddCommGroup E. The local-existence consumer additionally needs NormedSpace ℝ E and CompleteSpace E.
  - Supply actual closed-ball norm bound L:ℝ≥0, Lipschitz constant K:ℝ≥0, radii a,r:ℝ≥0, and L*max(tmax−t₀,t₀−tmin)≤a−r.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Derive L and K for the actual F_(ν,n) on a radius ball, then choose the same restart interval for every point in the energy-controlled ball.

LIMITATIONS
  - Does not derive the vector-field estimates or existence by itself.
  - No finite-dimensional or compact-ball assumption occurs in this contract; do not import one from the older Methlib uniform_local_time theorem.

DECLARATIONS

  MV05.01 — IsPicardLindelof
  Kind: structure
  Source statement lines: 79–88
  Source signature:
    structure IsPicardLindelof {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
        (f : ℝ → E → E) {tmin tmax : ℝ} (t₀ : Icc tmin tmax) (x₀ : E) (a r L K : ℝ≥0) : Prop where
      /-- The vector field at any time is Lipschitz with constant `K` within a closed ball. -/
      lipschitzOnWith : ∀ t ∈ Icc tmin tmax, LipschitzOnWith K (f t) (closedBall x₀ a)
      /-- The vector field is continuous in time within a closed ball. -/
      continuousOn : ∀ x ∈ closedBall x₀ a, ContinuousOn (f · x) (Icc tmin tmax)
      /-- `L` is an upper bound of the norm of the vector field. -/
      norm_le : ∀ t ∈ Icc tmin tmax, ∀ x ∈ closedBall x₀ a, ‖f t x‖ ≤ L
      /-- The time interval of validity -/
      mul_max_le : L * max (tmax - t₀) (t₀ - tmin) ≤ a - r
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

  MV05.02 — IsPicardLindelof.of_time_independent
  Kind: lemma
  Source statement lines: 662–667
  Source signature:
    lemma of_time_independent
        {f : E → E} {tmin tmax : ℝ} {t₀ : Icc tmin tmax} {x₀ : E} {a r L K : ℝ≥0}
        (hb : ∀ x ∈ closedBall x₀ a, ‖f x‖ ≤ L)
        (hl : LipschitzOnWith K f (closedBall x₀ a))
        (hm : L * max (tmax - t₀) (t₀ - tmin) ≤ a - r) :
        (IsPicardLindelof (fun _ ↦ f) t₀ x₀ a r L K)
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

EVIDENCE / SOURCE POINTERS
  https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Analysis/ODE/PicardLindelof.lean
    Exact immutable source inspected statically; declaration spans below refer to this file. No compiler or kernel was invoked.

==============================================================================

MV10 — Finite-dimensional global dissipative ODE solver: scope exclusion
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methlib
Revision: 96033dd69c994bec337a87369a6c27256e724980
Source path: regularity-B-assembly/Singular-Axiomatic-closure/SingularClosure/DissipativeODE.lean
Import module: SingularClosure.DissipativeODE
Git blob: 8f95c8e11a98951def0e9126a7d180288fbe32e0
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methlib/blob/96033dd69c994bec337a87369a6c27256e724980/regularity-B-assembly/Singular-Axiomatic-closure/SingularClosure/DissipativeODE.lean
Namespace: SingularClosure
Plan steps: I05, I06
Reuse classification: FINITE_DIMENSIONAL_SOURCE_TEMPLATE_NOT_DROP_IN
Evidence status:
  VENDORED_SOURCE_IDENTITY_VERIFIED_NO_NEW_FORMAL_ACCEPTANCE. No exact named type/axiom receipt was inspected for the declarations in this card; do not promote them solely from a package or dependency listing.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  In the finite-dimensional real Hilbert setting, C1 vector fields have a common local time on a radius ball; actual solutions agree on overlaps; dissipative fields have a forward-global solution.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Ambient declaration at source line31: {V:Type*}[NormedAddCommGroup V][InnerProductSpace ℝ V][FiniteDimensional ℝ V].
  - All three indexed public declarations retain the finite-dimensional setting; none has a preceding omit for its own declaration.
  - uniform_local_time and solution_agree use compact closed balls; solution_agree obtains its Lipschitz constant through isCompact_closedBall. The global theorem calls uniform_local_time.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Rebuild the global assembly for E_n with explicit ballwise RHS bounds and the dimension-free Mathlib existence/uniqueness tools. Do not instantiate this finite-dimensional global constructor on the whole-space bandlimited space.

LIMITATIONS

SC01 — Interior scalar cubic bootstrap
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/SmallForcingBootstrap.lean

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  A continuous scalar error with nonpositive initial value and a guarded cubic differential inequality stays at most one on a fixed closed slab once a strictly positive forcing sequence is sufficiently small. The eventual theorem requires derivatives only in the open interval.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - C ≥ 0; epsilon : ℕ → ℝ with eventually epsilon n > 0 and epsilon → 0.
  - For every n: ContinuousOn (D n) (Icc 0 T), D n 0 ≤ 0, and actual HasDerivAt at every t ∈ Ioo 0 T.
  - The upper derivative inequality is required when D n t ≤ 1. No global nonnegativity hypothesis appears in this consumer; the constructed squared H1 error is nevertheless nonnegative.
  - The single-forcing theorem additionally requires 2 * epsilon * exp ((2*C+1)*T) ≤ 1.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Instantiate D with the full R3 H1 error square and its actual interior derivative.
  - Use epsilon_n = Kforce * residualBound_n^2 + 1/(n+1) to satisfy strict positivity even when the residual vanishes.
  - Supply the n-independent constant and closed-slab continuity proved from the actual cutoff/reference paths.

LIMITATIONS
  - It supplies no PDE, physical product estimate, reference construction, or derivative identity.
  - The at-zero within-derivative versions elsewhere in the module have stronger boundary requirements; this card selects the interior versions.

SC02 — Terminal descent and positive-terminal contradiction
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/SlabDescent.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.SlabDescent
Git blob: 3998dd3f9b548ef9aea50a8e6fc9369ad465051a
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/SlabDescent.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I08, I15
Reuse classification: DIRECT_SCALAR_REUSE
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED IN MAIN AT EXISTING SCOPE: source blob matches tested integration 5a44e985c15a302110fd91d064431f1183e55857; retained named type/axiom evidence and integration comparison preserved. Historical 'prepared/pending' source comments do not supersede the matching acceptance receipt. No new theorem or audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  An explicit interior derivative bounded above by -gamma forces v(T) ≤ v(0)-gamma*T. If the initial value is at most gamma*T and the terminal value is positive, the premises contradict each other.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - T > 0; v and rate are real functions on ℝ; v is continuous on Icc 0 T.
  - HasDerivAt v (rate t) t and rate t ≤ -gamma for all t ∈ Ioo 0 T.
  - For the contradiction theorem: v 0 ≤ gamma*T and 0 < v T. Gamma positivity is not a separate theorem premise.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Set T=1/gamma and v to the canonical R3 reciprocal; prove gamma*T=1 from gamma>0.
  - Obtain the rate bound from the stopped predicate after choosing one cutoff whose threshold exceeds the whole-slab enstrophy bound.

LIMITATIONS
  - This theorem has no stopping guard and does not select a cutoff.
  - Continuity at the terminal time is essential; positive-time/preterminal smoothness alone does not instantiate it.

DECLARATIONS

SC03 — Scalar reciprocal threshold equivalences
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/ReciprocalThreshold.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.ReciprocalThreshold
Git blob: 2b307b3bd0763575464913f2eef5109aa869bb06
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/ReciprocalThreshold.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I07, I08
Reuse classification: DIRECT_SCALAR_REUSE_OPTIONAL_THRESHOLD_ADAPTER
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED IN MAIN AT EXISTING SCOPE: source blob matches tested integration 5a44e985c15a302110fd91d064431f1183e55857; retained named type/axiom evidence and integration comparison preserved. Historical 'prepared/pending' source comments do not supersede the matching acceptance receipt. No new theorem or audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For positive eStar and nonnegative e,R, the reciprocal threshold is positive; e<R, R≤e, and e=R are exactly equivalent to the corresponding reverse-order reciprocal comparisons.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - eStar,e,R : ℝ with eStar>0, e≥0, and R≥0 as shown in each declaration.
  - The source reciprocal is (1+e/eStar)⁻¹.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Prove once that eStar/(eStar+e) = (1+e/eStar)⁻¹ under eStar>0 and e≥0.
  - Instantiate e with the physical half-enstrophy and R with the actual cutoff threshold.

LIMITATIONS
  - These are scalar algebra only; no first hitting time, drift, or growth statement follows from them.
  - The one-cutoff descent proof can proceed without reciprocal-threshold conversion.

DECLARATIONS

  SC03.01 — MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.reciprocal_threshold_pos
  Kind: theorem
  Source statement lines: 22–24
  Source signature:
    theorem reciprocal_threshold_pos
        (eStar R : ℝ) (heStar : 0 < eStar) (hR : 0 ≤ R) :

SC04 — Pointwise transport and Hamiltonian commutator algebra
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CoreAlgebra.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.CoreAlgebra
Git blob: 85fd00b5db5bb1311b3523d9cc0c59a85d457fc2
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CoreAlgebra.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I07, I08
Reuse classification: OPTIONAL_POINTWISE_ALGEBRA
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED IN MAIN AT EXISTING SCOPE: source blob matches tested integration 5a44e985c15a302110fd91d064431f1183e55857; retained named type/axiom evidence and integration comparison preserved. Historical 'prepared/pending' source comments do not supersede the matching acceptance receipt. No new theorem or audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The transport-minus-multiplication commutator equals the directional derivative of the multiplier. The negative-i Hamiltonian convention yields the exact normalized commutator after cancelling positive hbar.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Implicit source context: {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]. No FiniteDimensional or CompleteSpace assumption occurs.
  - b : E → E; z,g,ψ : E → ℂ; DifferentiableAt ℝ g x and DifferentiableAt ℝ ψ x.
  - The normalized theorem requires hbar : ℝ with 0<hbar.
  - transportExpression b z f x = fderiv ℝ f x (b x) + z x * f x; hamiltonianExpression = -Complex.I * (hbar : ℂ) * transportExpression.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Use E equal to the complete bandlimited real phase space, b equal to the actual nonlinear vector field, and g equal to the complex embedding of the reciprocal observable.
  - Prove actual differentiability of the reciprocal and its directional/time-derivative identification.

LIMITATIONS
  - This is optional interpretation; it is not needed for the scalar conditional root.
  - It provides no phase-space measure, L2 operator domain, self-adjoint Hamiltonian, unitary group, or full Hilbert-space Ehrenfest theorem.

DECLARATIONS

        transportExpression b z (fun y => g y * ψ y) x -
            g x * transportExpression b z ψ x =
          fderiv ℝ g x (b x) * ψ x

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Actual physical H1 weak gradients satisfy full-volume integration by parts; actual H2 Laplacians obey the H1-H2 Green pairing, a bilinear symmetry identity, and pairing with velocity equal to minus the full physical H1 enstrophy.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Actual PhysicalH1 and PhysicalH2 graph elements; no solenoidality or solution-path hypothesis is required.
  - The relevant actual velocity, weak-gradient, and Laplacian maps in QuasiFeffermanA.
  - All spatial inner products are the actual real SpatialL2 inner products.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Transfer the exact accepted source and dependency closure into the intended branch before using its imports.
  - Admit cutoff states and their time derivatives to the physical H1/H2 graphs.
  - Derive nonlinear transport cancellation and pathwise differentiation separately.

LIMITATIONS
  - physicalH1Enstrophy is the full gradient norm squared; the new e_n is half of it.
  - The bilinear Laplacian symmetry is not an unbounded-operator self-adjointness theorem.
  - This file has no nonlinear existence, forced time derivative, or time-energy theorem.

SC08 — Optional free-heat generator and integrated equation
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: 21b4ae85ed3009bc623fbfe882de9ec932bac9f5
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/HeatGenerator.lean

Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED AT EXACT CROSS-BRANCH SOURCE: externally tested 809aadc6da1eeabd5c128eb9c78005a38d3d4935; identical blob at AC21b4ae85. Existing named reports use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. This source is not already imported from frozen main b4218fbc; integration remains separate.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The actual free L2 heat path is continuous; for physical H1 data and positive viscosity it has strong L2 derivative nu times its actual Laplacian at positive times, a finite-window L2 time derivative, and the exact integrated equation from the original datum.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - ν : ℝ≥0; generator/integrability/integrated-equation theorems further require hν : 0<ν.
  - a : PhysicalH1; positive time for HasDerivAt; T≥0 for the finite-window conclusions.
  - The time-L2 measure is volume.restrict (Ioc 0 T).

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Coerce a positive real viscosity to ℝ≥0 while preserving its value.
  - Use this only when developing a separate genuine mild/reference-admission route; it is optional for the direct C_t H2 comparison.
  - Preserve the original heatL2 value at zero when identifying the initial trace.

LIMITATIONS
  - These are free linear heat theorems and do not construct a nonlinear NSE reference.
  - The H2 heatTrajectory uses a zero extension away from positive times; do not replace the original L2 initial value by that extension.
  - No forced Duhamel derivative or forced maximal regularity is supplied.

SC09 — Optional actual L2 Duhamel response
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: 21b4ae85ed3009bc623fbfe882de9ec932bac9f5
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/HeatDuhamel.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.HeatDuhamel
Git blob: 23cf6e677389215c5958efd2d2c95b0d99b2d199
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/21b4ae85ed3009bc623fbfe882de9ec932bac9f5/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/HeatDuhamel.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.HeatDuhamel
Plan steps: I10, I14
Reuse classification: ACCEPTED_CROSS_BRANCH_OPTIONAL_FORCED_L2_RESPONSE
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED AT EXACT CROSS-BRANCH SOURCE: externally tested 9b8250be2f13048e3e21cd94c8ba1939d919bf64; identical blob at AC21b4ae85. Existing named reports use only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. This source is not already imported from frozen main b4218fbc; integration remains separate.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs the retarded Bochner integral of the actual heat semigroup; proves forcing a.e.-class invariance, closed-slab L2 continuity, and the sqrt(T) bound using the actual time-L2 forcing quotient norm.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - ν : ℝ≥0; f : ℝ → SpatialL2.
  - For continuity and norm estimates: MemLp f 2 (volume.restrict (Ioc 0 T)), T≥0, and 0≤t≤T as specified.
  - A.e. invariance uses equality on Ioc 0 T and times within the corresponding closed slab.
  - duhamelL2 ν f t is defined as ∫ s in 0..t, HeatSemigroup.heatL2 ν (t-s).toNNReal (f s).

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Supply an actual time-L2 spatial-L2 forcing representative; identify any proposed residual with that forcing.
  - Import the accepted dependency closure if taking a future Duhamel route.
  - For the selected direct error-energy route, no use of this optional response is required.

LIMITATIONS
  - The proved forcing class is time L2, not a general arbitrary time-L1 admission.
  - The construction and bounds do not prove the inhomogeneous time-derivative equation or forced H1/H2 maximal regularity.
  - A total Bochner-integral definition alone is not a nonlinear solution theorem.

==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke

Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/21b4ae85ed3009bc623fbfe882de9ec932bac9f5/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/HeatDuhamelOperator.lean

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs a real continuous linear map from the finite-window time-L2 SpatialL2 forcing quotient to continuous SpatialL2 paths, with operator norm at most sqrt(T), zero initial trace, and compatibility with every actual MemLp representative.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - ν : ℝ≥0, T : ℝ, hT : 0≤T.
  - Domain: MeasureTheory.Lp SpatialL2 2 (volume.restrict (Ioc 0 T)); codomain: ContinuousMap (Icc 0 T) SpatialL2.
  - For the representative theorem: f : ℝ → SpatialL2, its actual MemLp proof, and t : Icc 0 T.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Translate a legitimate forcing representative to its actual toLp class and use the supplied compatibility equality.
  - Keep this an optional mild-response tool; it is not needed to instantiate the full H1 scalar bootstrap already derived.

LIMITATIONS
  - The codomain is continuous L2 paths, not C_t H1 or a maximal-regularity space.
  - Linearity and the sqrt(T) estimate do not furnish the forced time derivative or solve the nonlinear fixed-point problem.

AN01 — Actual Holder product induced by a continuous bilinear map
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/MeasureTheory/Function/Holder.lean
Import module: Mathlib.MeasureTheory.Function.Holder
Git blob: 28a09ce608dfff93cf2006587836e591a9f7dff2

Namespace: ContinuousLinearMap
Plan steps: I04, I09, I11
Reuse classification: DIRECT_LP_PRODUCT_CONSTRUCTION_WITH_PHYSICAL_ADAPTER
Evidence status:
  PINNED_MATHLIB_SOURCE_INSPECTED_NO_NEW_NAMED_AUDIT. Exact source and Git blob at the project's dependency pin were inspected. No exact-name cached compiler/axiom receipt was newly inspected for the declarations in this card, and no A-tier promotion is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  A bounded bilinear map on the value spaces induces an actual Lp product in the Holder output exponent, with an a.e. representative formula and norm bound ||B.holder r f g|| <= ||B|| ||f|| ||g||. With exponents at least one, the construction is itself a continuous bilinear map between Lp spaces.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Ambient source lines 38–42: {α 𝕜 E F G : Type*}, measurable α and measure μ, exponents p q r : ENNReal with [ENNReal.HolderTriple p q r], [NontriviallyNormedField 𝕜], normed additive groups E,F,G and NormedSpace 𝕜 structures.
  - The supplied value-space map is B : E →L[𝕜] F →L[𝕜] G. memLp_of_bilin additionally takes the actual MemLp premises; its output exponent r is an explicit parameter from the preceding variable declaration.
  - holderL additionally uses [Fact (1 ≤ p)] [Fact (1 ≤ q)] [Fact (1 ≤ r)] at source line 120. Its displayed source signature retains implicit B and explicit μ,p,q,r through the local variable declarations.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Specialize B to scalar-vector multiplication or the required finite-coordinate bilinear contraction. Supply the actual spatial Lp memberships (for example exponents ∞,2,2 for bandlimited RHS control or 6,3,2 for H1/H2 products), identify representatives, and combine with J_n and the physical Leray projection.

LIMITATIONS
  - This constructs products only after both input memberships and the Holder exponent relation are established.
  - It does not prove Bernstein, Sobolev, H1-to-L6 or gradient-to-L3 admission, nor the spatial weak Leibniz rule.
  - The actual truncated convection sum, invariance of the bandlimited solenoidal carrier, and quantitative radius-ball Lipschitz bounds remain adapters.

==============================================================================

  The heterogeneous Lp scalar action has the literal pointwise scalar-vector product a.e. and satisfies ||f • g|| <= ||f|| ||g|| in the Holder output space.

AN03 — Real Young inequalities for dissipative absorption
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Analysis/MeanInequalities.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Analysis.MeanInequalities

Plan steps: I04, I09, I11
Reuse classification: DIRECT_SCALAR_INEQUALITY_WITH_WEIGHTED_SUBSTITUTION
Evidence status:

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For conjugate real exponents, bounds a product by the sum of the corresponding powered terms divided by their exponents. The first version assumes nonnegative factors; the second uses absolute values.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - The exponent relation is the actual hypothesis hpq : p.HolderConjugate q for p,q : ℝ.
  - young_inequality_of_nonneg requires 0 ≤ a and 0 ≤ b. young_inequality takes arbitrary real a,b and places absolute values on the right.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Choose the conjugate exponents required by the proved product estimate, rescale the two nonnegative norm factors by a positive viscosity-dependent weight, and explicitly simplify the resulting powers to reserve the needed portion of viscous dissipation.

LIMITATIONS
  - No weighted epsilon/viscosity coefficient is built into these two declarations; every rescaling, positivity premise and coefficient simplification remains explicit.
  - They do not establish the product estimate being absorbed, a cutoff-independent constant, or the final cubic error inequality.
  - Using these source anchors does not discharge the separate sharp-QFA Sobolev constant obligation.

AN04 — Inner-product time derivative rules
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Analysis/InnerProductSpace/Calculus.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Analysis.InnerProductSpace.Calculus
Git blob: 0cb732b074509d4aa9fccd3c3f83d30f9021eb90
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Analysis/InnerProductSpace/Calculus.lean
Namespace: _root_ (HasDerivAt and HasDerivWithinAt namespaces)
Plan steps: I06, I07, I11
Reuse classification: DIRECT_HILBERT_TIME_PRODUCT_RULE
Evidence status:
  PINNED_MATHLIB_SOURCE_INSPECTED_NO_NEW_NAMED_AUDIT. Exact source and Git blob at the project's dependency pin were inspected. No exact-name cached compiler/axiom receipt was newly inspected for the declarations in this card, and no A-tier promotion is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Differentiates an inner product of two actual differentiable time curves, yielding the ordered sum <f(t),g'> + <f',g(t)>; both ordinary and within-set derivative versions are available.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Source section: {𝕜 E : Type*} [RCLike 𝕜] [NormedAddCommGroup E] [InnerProductSpace 𝕜 E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]. The explicit scalar parameter 𝕜 is introduced at line 66.
  - Time is ℝ. Supply HasDerivAt (or HasDerivWithinAt on the same s) for both curves at the same time.
  - For the physical energy/enstrophy application, specialize 𝕜=ℝ and E to the appropriate actual L2 Hilbert space.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Transport the regularized-state derivatives into the actual velocity and gradient coordinates, then apply the rule to energy, enstrophy, and comparison pairings. Preserve the within-set version when treating an endpoint.

LIMITATIONS
  - The derivative hypotheses are substantive: these rules do not infer them from weak NSE momentum tests or from pointwise spatial smoothness.
  - They do not commute a time derivative with an unbounded spatial gradient/Laplacian; the bandlimited derivative lift or another valid time-regularity argument is required.
  - The displayed inner-product order must be retained for complex-valued intermediate fields; specialize or simplify only with the appropriate real/complex identities.

AN05 — Norm-square derivatives including interval endpoints and zero error
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Analysis/InnerProductSpace/Calculus.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Analysis.InnerProductSpace.Calculus
Git blob: 0cb732b074509d4aa9fccd3c3f83d30f9021eb90
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Analysis/InnerProductSpace/Calculus.lean
Namespace: _root_ (HasDerivAt and HasDerivWithinAt namespaces)
Plan steps: I06, I07, I11
Reuse classification: DIRECT_REAL_HILBERT_ENERGY_CHAIN_RULE
Evidence status:
  PINNED_MATHLIB_SOURCE_INSPECTED_NO_NEW_NAMED_AUDIT. Exact source and Git blob at the project's dependency pin were inspected. No exact-name cached compiler/axiom receipt was newly inspected for the declarations in this card, and no A-tier promotion is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For a real Hilbert-valued differentiable curve, gives the exact derivative of its squared norm as 2*<f(t),f'>. The same formula holds for the derivative within a set, without a nonzero-norm assumption.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - Source lines 39–41 and the real-inner-product section at 194–195: {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] [InnerProductSpace ℝ F]. No finite-dimensional or completeness premise is required by these derivative statements.
  - Supply the actual HasDerivAt or HasDerivWithinAt hypothesis for f : ℝ → F, with derivative f' : F; the within version retains its exact set s.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Apply to the actual L2 error and its three physical gradient-error curves, add the resulting finite scalar identities, and use the proved weak derivative/Laplacian pairings to obtain the full H1 energy derivative. For half-energy, multiply the exact identity by 1/2.

LIMITATIONS
  - These are norm-square rules, not derivatives of the norm. They remain valid when the initial error is zero.
  - The within-set result can match the closed-interval Picard derivative interface, but any later theorem requiring full HasDerivAt still needs its own compatible endpoint or local-extension argument.
  - They do not establish nonlinear cancellation, viscosity signs, error residual estimates, or the a.e./absolutely-continuous calculus for a merely weak trajectory.

EP01 — Deriving the actual periodic projected-error equation
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a

Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I10
Reuse classification: PERIODIC_EVOLUTION_PROOF_PATTERN
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED MAIN SOURCE AT ITS EXISTING SCOPE. The blob matches externally accepted integration 5a44e985; all selected names occur in the retained named reports and integration comparison with only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Historical prepared/pending source comments are superseded by that source-specific receipt. No new theorem, compiler run, or kernel audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The algebraic RHS identity identifies the exact finite error forcing, and the final theorem derives the coefficient derivative of an actual Galerkin solution minus a projected classical reference. The final theorem does not assume an error evolution certificate.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - For finite_error_rhs_identity: smooth U, coefficient decomposition c=w+b, spectral support and reality for c and w, and a retained frequency k ∈ cutoffModes N.
  - For initial_front_galerkin_error_hasDerivAt: an actual InitialClassicalFront ν S a u p, actual GalerkinSolution ν a N, and t ∈ Ioo 0 S.
  - Frequency, FourierCoeff, GalerkinSolution, cutoffCoeff and galerkinRhs are the existing SingularClosure finite periodic Fourier constructions.
  - finiteReferenceErrorForcing equals B(U,U)-B(u,u)+B(w,w) when u=w+v. Its forcing is added to the nonlinear RHS for w.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Derive u_n' minus J_n U' directly on the R3 bandlimited Hilbert carrier, using the actually constructed u_n and projected reference PDE.
  - Prove the cutoff/Leray commutations and identify the actual physical residual; do not assign a GalerkinSolution instance to the error.
  - With rho=v-U and r_n=J_n P(B(v,v)-B(U,U)), the R3 error equation contains -r_n. The source residual B(U,U)-B(v,v) has the opposite sign.

LIMITATIONS
  - The coefficientwise HasDerivAt conclusion is not a whole-space H1-valued derivative theorem.
  - The source finite frequency cutoff is not the infinite-dimensional R3 ball multiplier.
  - Pressure elimination and reference evolution are already established for the periodic front imported here; their whole-space admission remains a separate obligation.

DECLARATIONS

    theorem finite_error_rhs_identity
        (ν : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (U : Field) (c b w : FourierCoeff)
        (hU : ContDiff ℝ ∞ U) (hsum : c = w + b)
        (hcsupp : ∀ k : Frequency, k ∉ cutoffModes N → ∀ i : Fin 3, c k i = 0)
        (hcreal : SpectralReality c)
        (hwsupp : ∀ k : Frequency, k ∉ cutoffModes N → ∀ i : Fin 3, w k i = 0)

        galerkinRhs ν N c k i -
            (-((ν * (2 * Real.pi) ^ 2 * (galerkinFreqSq k : ℝ) : ℝ) : ℂ) * b k i -

          galerkinRhs ν N w k i +
            cutoffCoeff N (fun l => coefficientLeray l
              (datumFourierCoeff (finiteReferenceErrorForcing N U b w) l)) k i

==============================================================================
EP02 — Finite error differentiation, exact balance and cubic absorption
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CoefficientError.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.CoefficientError
Git blob: d5dd6d5a400228f16e9dd54b1373356970a8e722
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CoefficientError.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I07, I11
Reuse classification: FINITE_PERIODIC_CALCULUS_AND_ABSORPTION_PATTERN
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED MAIN SOURCE AT ITS EXISTING SCOPE. The blob matches externally accepted integration 5a44e985; all selected names occur in the retained named reports and integration comparison with only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Historical prepared/pending source comments are superseded by that source-specific receipt. No new theorem, compiler run, or kernel audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Actual coordinate derivatives differentiate the finite gradient-energy square and its difference curve. A literal forced coordinate equation gives the exact viscous/nonlinear/forcing balance. Spectrally supported real solenoidal coefficients satisfy a cutoff-independent cubic self-interaction absorption bound.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - The derivative and exact-balance statements need actual coordinatewise HasDerivWithinAt hypotheses on the stated time set; they do not require a GalerkinSolution record for the error.
  - The exact balance uses any real ν; positivity of ν is required by FINITE_ERROR_NONLINEAR_ABSORPTION.
  - Nonlinear absorption additionally requires finite spectral support, SpectralReality and SpectralSolenoidal.
  - dissipationRatePairing is twice the real coefficient pairing with positive Laplacian weights. coefficientDissipation is the full gradient-energy square, not half-enstrophy.
  - In the exact balance f is the forcing value at the tested time; repeated application may use different f(t), so no global constant-in-time forcing restriction is intended.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Replace differentiation of a finite sum with differentiation of the actual full physical H1 norm along C1_t H1 cutoff/reference paths.
  - Prove the whole-space nonlinear estimate from the admitted Sobolev/Gagliardo-Nirenberg products and Young inequality, retaining cutoff-independent constants.
  - Preserve the normalization: the source identity is D'=-2ν Z-2 nonlinearPower+forcingPairing, while the plan separately defines e_n=(1/2)||grad u_n||².

LIMITATIONS
  - The source module is CoefficientError.lean; EhrenfestErrorCalculus is its audit package, not a source module named ErrorCalculus.lean.
  - The absorption proof imports a finite periodic physical nonlinear bound. Its mathematical role transfers; the theorem cannot be applied directly to R3 cutoff functions.
  - Gradient energy alone is not the whole-space full H1 error norm. These statements do not provide the missing L2 error estimate.

DECLARATIONS

==============================================================================
EP03 — Removing projections from finite forcing pairings
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/PhysicalForcing.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.PhysicalForcing
Git blob: d3944d71cc66752b4a7ff8901e41c171818b36c3
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/PhysicalForcing.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I06, I07, I11
Reuse classification: PERIODIC_PROJECTION_AND_PHYSICAL_PAIRING_PATTERN
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED MAIN SOURCE AT ITS EXISTING SCOPE. The blob matches externally accepted integration 5a44e985; all selected names occur in the retained named reports and integration comparison with only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Historical prepared/pending source comments are superseded by that source-specific receipt. No new theorem, compiler run, or kernel audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The finite gradient pairing is unchanged by cutoff projection, and by coefficient Leray projection when the test coefficients are solenoidal. Its Fourier forcing pairing equals -2 times the literal unit-cube pairing with the physical Laplacian. Young absorption then gives the physical-forcing enstrophy inequality.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - dissipationRatePairing_cutoff_right has no solenoidality premise; dissipationRatePairing_leray_right requires SpectralSolenoidal c.
  - The Fourier-to-physical identity requires F : Field with Continuous F and concludes an integral over unitCube.
  - The projected forcing bound requires δ>0, SpectralReality c, SpectralSolenoidal c, and Continuous F.
  - The full inequality requires ν>0, actual supported/real/solenoidal state coefficients at t, and the literal forced coordinate derivative equation on the stated time set.
  - The source bound retains an actual integral of ||F||²; no abstract forcing-norm certificate substitutes for it.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - For R3, prove that w_n and Delta w_n lie in the range of the commuting self-adjoint J_n P, then remove these projections from actual SpatialL2 pairings.
  - Use the accepted whole-space physical Green identities and genuine global L2 integrability to replace the unit-cube Fourier identity.
  - Supply the actual projected error equation and derive the R3 full H1 forcing estimates; retain the separate L2 pairing term as well as the Laplacian pairing.

LIMITATIONS
  - Continuity ensures the relevant integrability on the compact unit cube; it does not establish L2 integrability on all of R3.
  - The factor -2 is tied to differentiation of the full gradient norm square.
  - Projection removal is not a proof of nonlinear transport cancellation; the R3 divergence-free cancellation still needs its legitimate full-volume argument.
  - The concluding ν/2 dissipative coefficient and 2/ν forcing coefficient belong to this periodic balance and are not the constants of the plan's full H1 estimate.

Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CrossTransport.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I10, I11
Reuse classification: POINTWISE_ALGEBRA_WITH_PERIODIC_NORM_ASSEMBLY_PATTERN
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED MAIN SOURCE AT ITS EXISTING SCOPE. The blob matches externally accepted integration 5a44e985; all selected names occur in the retained named reports and integration comparison with only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Historical prepared/pending source comments are superseded by that source-specific receipt. No new theorem, compiler run, or kernel audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Pointwise convection expansion isolates the two cross transports. The residual for U-v is exactly B(U,U)-B(v,v). A unit-cube C1 reference estimate bounds that residual by the tail's L2-plus-gradient square, and the periodic finite error equation yields a cubic inequality with explicit reference and residual terms.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - physicalTransport a b x = fderiv ℝ b x (a x). The pointwise expansion and residual identity require the stated ContDiff ℝ ∞ representatives; their signatures add no separate periodicity hypothesis.
  - physicalReferenceResidual U v r = B(r,U)+B(v,r); for r=U-v it equals B(U,U)-B(v,v).
  - The residual L2 bound requires uniform bounds on ||fderiv U|| and ||v|| over unitCube; it is not an estimate assuming only an H2 norm.
  - The final cross inequality additionally requires C1 supremum bounds for v, a continuous residual R, spectral support/reality/solenoidality, and the literal forced coefficient equation.
  - physicalErrorForcing v w R = R-B(v,w)-B(w,v).

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Reuse the pointwise identities only after identifying the actual smooth representatives and their derivative conventions; do not transfer a cube norm conclusion as a whole-volume one.
  - Keep the source residual sign distinct: after J_n P projection it is -r_n in the plan's rho=v-U convention.
  - Replace the source C1 supremum estimates by the plan's L6×L3 and Linfinity×L2 bounds from a C_t H2 reference.
  - Retain D_n=||w_n||²+||grad w_n||² in R3. The periodic cross estimate folds the L2 error into gradient energy, which the whole-space proof must not do.

LIMITATIONS
  - At source line 214, finite_physicalErrorForcing_l2_le explicitly uses coefficientEnergy_le_dissipation; this periodic spectral-gap step feeds the final cross inequality.
  - Continuous R3 frequencies near zero prevent importing that spectral gap. Removing only the single zero frequency does not create an R3 Poincare inequality.
  - C_t H2 on R3 does not imply a uniform Linfinity bound on grad U; the existing cube residual estimate therefore cannot be substituted for the plan's H2 residual proof.
  - The final constants 24*M²/ν and 6/ν are exact for the periodic assembly, not a certification of the new R3 constants.

EP05 — Periodic end-to-end uniform cubic-error assembly
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CanonicalErrorInequality.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction.CanonicalErrorInequality
Git blob: ecc274bb051279fbcba5d30e7ebe4aa310ec601f
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/EhrenfestObstruction/CanonicalErrorInequality.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.EhrenfestObstruction
Plan steps: I10, I11, I12
Reuse classification: PERIODIC_FINAL_ASSEMBLY_PROOF_PATTERN
Evidence status:
  ACCEPTED MAIN SOURCE AT ITS EXISTING SCOPE. The blob matches externally accepted integration 5a44e985; all selected names occur in the retained named reports and integration comparison with only propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound. Historical prepared/pending source comments are superseded by that source-specific receipt. No new theorem, compiler run, or kernel audit is claimed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  An actual periodic smooth initial front on a larger time interval supplies positive constants C,A independent of n and t, an actual interior derivative of the canonical error's gradient-energy square, and the bound d≤C(D+D³)+A/(n+1)².

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  - ν>0, the existing periodic SmoothDatum, an actual InitialClassicalFront ν S a.val u p, and the strict closed-slab margin T<S.
  - The same datum and canonical Galerkin family are used at every cutoff n+1.
  - The theorem derives reference C1 bounds, physical H1 truncation tails, the actual error equation, and the uniform cubic estimate from its front hypothesis; it does not take those as extra assumed ports.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  - Reproduce the assembly order with the new R3 construction: actual projected equation, compact-trajectory H1 tails, global L2 residual, and full H1 differential inequality.
  - Supply strictly positive epsilon_n tending to zero when invoking the already indexed scalar interior bootstrap; the plan's residual-square plus 1/(n+1) is sufficient.
  - Keep the temporary C_t H2 reference lemma separate from the literal NSE-A admission theorem.

LIMITATIONS
  - The stated coefficientDissipation and canonicalErrorCoeff remain periodic and omit the whole-space low-frequency L2 error term.
  - The explicit A/(n+1)² tail rate and its constants are proved for the periodic construction; a whole-space rate, if desired, requires its own proof.
  - This accepted periodic derivative estimate is not an accepted R3 E09 theorem or an admission of literal whole-space NSE-A solutions.
