EX01 — Accepted squared-energy interpolation from actual spectral witnesses
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/_vendor/QFA/Assembly/AnalyticChain.lean
Import module: QFA.Assembly.AnalyticChain
Git blob: 3893ab9916d6553d23a71df8f13bcbe3d1a9fdf5
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/_vendor/QFA/Assembly/AnalyticChain.lean
Namespace: QFA.Assembly.AnalyticChain
Plan steps: I07, I09, I11
Reuse classification: Existing accepted conditional theorem construction; short physical-carrier adapter remains
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact current vendor module bytes match the module at accepted integration 5a44e985, and both named theorem types/axioms are retained under QFARecovered. The source-era candidate header does not override that later scoped evidence. No new physical wrapper or I09/I11 endpoint is accepted by this index.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For actual complex vector L2 fields v, directional derivatives G and Laplacian L with NativeSpectralRealization, proves (sum_j ||G_j||_2^2)^2 <= ||v||_2^2 ||L||_2^2. The endpoint wrapper converts any supplied WholeSpaceSpectralEndpoint into WholeSpaceInterpolationEndpoint. The proof uses the nonnegative weighted Fourier quadratic and includes zero-energy cases without division.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  NativeSpectralRealization contains three integrability assertions and three exact identities for velocity, gradient and Laplacian energies, with weights 1, (2*pi)^2|xi|^2 and (2*pi)^4|xi|^4. Equivalently use the second theorem with a proved spectral endpoint; EX02 supplies that endpoint from Schwartz-distribution derivative witnesses. No Agmon hypothesis enters these two statements.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  For a native PhysicalH2 field, compose its existing physicalH2_schwartzDerivativeWitnesses with EX02, then rewrite the complexified velocity/derivative/Laplacian energies using the real-to-complex L2 isometry. This yields the physical interpolation estimate needed in I09/I11 estimates. The composed native convenience theorem is not constructed or externally checked here.

LIMITATIONS
  This is quadratic L2 interpolation, not an H2-to-L-infinity or H1-to-L6 theorem, and it does not bound convection by itself. It supplies no cutoff tail estimate, trajectory, pressure identity or time derivative. I09 still requires quantitative physical Sobolev/Agmon and product estimates; I11 still requires the actual error pairing and temporal identities.

EX02 — Accepted native spectral realization from distributional derivatives
==============================================================================
Repository: randogelist/Methboke
Revision: b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a
Source path: Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/Spectral.lean
Import module: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA.Spectral
Git blob: fa22836312aa39a4e1896a4f8267d0988b713d8d
Source URL: https://github.com/randogelist/Methboke/blob/b4218fbcdd130189b69b1ee3f6df81b036553e4a/Material/MethbokeAddon/QuasiFeffermanA/Spectral.lean
Namespace: MethbokeAddon.QuasiFeffermanA
Plan steps: I07, I09, I10, I11
Reuse classification: Existing accepted theorem constructing all spectral outputs from supplied actual derivatives
Evidence status:
  EXISTING_ACCEPTED_SCOPE: exact current source bytes match accepted integration 5a44e985 and the earlier source-specific afd67db4/d403c235 receipts. Both named theorem type/axiom records are preserved under QFANativeFoundation. The conclusion is scoped to the supplied actual derivative witnesses; no physical PDE solution is constructed.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs NativeSpectralRealization from SchwartzDerivativeWitnesses: genuine Fourier energy integrability and exact velocity/gradient/Laplacian energy identities. It also proves WholeSpaceSpectralEndpoint, universally over actual complex L2 velocity, three L2 derivative fields and an L2 Laplacian satisfying the stated tempered-distribution equalities. This discharges EX01's spectral premise rather than leaving it as a newly assumed endpoint.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Supply SchwartzDerivativeWitnesses v G L: for each coordinate j, the tempered distribution of G_j equals the directional derivative of v; the tempered distribution of L equals the Laplacian of v. The fields themselves must already be actual ComplexL2 values. There is no extra arbitrary integrability or Fourier-energy assumption once these derivative witnesses are supplied.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  The existing physicalH2_schwartzDerivativeWitnesses theorem produces these witnesses for complexified native PhysicalH2 fields. Use it with this card and the physical H1/H2 spectral identities already indexed in PC11/PC12. For I10, constructing the actual sharp cutoff J_N, proving its derivative commutation, and integrating the high-frequency weight inequality remain separate new adapters; this card does not itself yield a tail rate.

LIMITATIONS
  Derivative fields are supplied, not constructed from an arbitrary global smooth finite-energy NSE_A path. The result does not establish physical H1/H2 admission on closed slabs, L-infinity representatives, nonlinear estimates, ODE solutions or pressure elimination. WholeSpaceSpectralEndpoint has fewer conclusions than the separate WholeSpaceAgmonEndpoint.

DECLARATIONS

  Kind: theorem
  Source statement lines: 102–105

==============================================================================
EX03 — External nonsharp Schwartz Sobolev estimate and simultaneous H1 approximation
==============================================================================
Repository: uda-lab/leray-hopf
Revision: e704400f2fb2f26b2ee7f4372c3e1ecbbc82f3dc
Source path: LerayHopf/R3/SobolevEmbedding.lean
Import module: LerayHopf.R3.SobolevEmbedding
Git blob: 8c90ad798d2cb62a06333992da12e0378c4b7811
Source URL: https://github.com/uda-lab/leray-hopf/blob/e704400f2fb2f26b2ee7f4372c3e1ecbbc82f3dc/LerayHopf/R3/SobolevEmbedding.lean
Namespace: LerayHopf
Plan steps: I09, I11
Reuse classification: Pinned external proof templates; quantitative Schwartz estimate, H1 membership and derivative-preserving approximation
Evidence status:
  EXTERNAL_PINNED_SOURCE_ONLY: exact public source signatures and bodies were read at the original pinned revision, but no matching native compiler/type/axiom evidence is retained. Upstream uses Lean 4.31.0-rc2 and Mathlib 15a89b24; native uses Lean 4.33.0 and Mathlib db584cd6. These statements are not native accepted declarations or newly verified proofs.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  The first public statement gives a quantitative homogeneous nonsharp L6 bound for complex scalar Schwartz functions, with Mathlib's SNormLESNormFDerivOfEqConst and the L2 norm of the Frechet derivative. The second concludes L6 membership for an actual complex L2 function with MemSobolev 1 2. The third supplies one Schwartz sequence converging in L2 to that function and, for every direction, in L2 to its weak derivative; the existential sequence precedes the universal direction.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  gns_L6_schwartz takes a SchwartzMap Domain3 C. The two H1 statements take f : L2C_R3 and hf : MemSobolev 1 2 (f : tempered distribution). Domain3, L2C_R3, the Bessel/Sobolev normalization, real directional derivatives and the scalar complex carrier are inherited from the original imports. There are no hidden section variables in the selected source signatures.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  For I09, first identify native PhysicalH1 components and their compact-test weak derivatives with the external spectral/Bessel H1 carrier, preserving the 2*pi normalization. Port the quantitative Schwartz estimate and common-sequence approximation, identify limiting weak derivatives, and pass the estimate to physical H1 with a quantitative limit/Fatou argument. Compare the operator norm of the Frechet derivative with the native sum of coordinate L2 derivative norms, then assemble the three real vector components. Apply the resulting bound to native PhysicalH2 gradients and interpolate L2/L6 when deriving the L3 products used in I11.

LIMITATIONS
  The public gns_L6_of_memH1_R3 conclusion is membership only; it must not be cited as the needed quantitative physical H1 bound. The common-sequence theorem gives convergence, not an accepted identification with the native physical graph carrier. The indexed Schwartz estimate is nonsharp; a finite nonsharp constant suffices for the plan and no sharp-S6 claim follows. H2-to-L-infinity, nonlinear H1 product bounds, actual residual estimates and error evolution remain new work.

DECLARATIONS

  Source signature:
    theorem gns_L6_schwartz (φ : SchwartzMap Domain3 ℂ) :

          SNormLESNormFDerivOfEqConst ℂ (volume : Measure Domain3) 2 *
            eLpNorm (fderiv ℝ (φ : Domain3 → ℂ)) 2 (volume : Measure Domain3)

==============================================================================
EX04 — External whole-space H1 convection integrability and spatial cancellation
==============================================================================
Repository: uda-lab/leray-hopf
Revision: e704400f2fb2f26b2ee7f4372c3e1ecbbc82f3dc
Source path: LerayHopf/R3/EnergyClassConvection.lean
Import module: LerayHopf.R3.EnergyClassConvection
Git blob: 9198e87ed306a0138299844a7cc4c9c2ca366ffa
Source URL: https://github.com/uda-lab/leray-hopf/blob/e704400f2fb2f26b2ee7f4372c3e1ecbbc82f3dc/LerayHopf/R3/EnergyClassConvection.lean
Namespace: LerayHopf
Plan steps: I06, I09, I11
Reuse classification: Pinned external spatial H1 proof templates; preserve explicit solenoidal premises
Evidence status:
  EXTERNAL_PINNED_SOURCE_ONLY: exact public source signatures and bodies were read at the pinned upstream revision. Retained ES14/ES15 cards supply provenance, not native compiler/type/axiom acceptance. Toolchain and Mathlib differ from native; no new external verification was requested or run.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For three actual H1 vector fields, proves absolute integrability of each u_a (partial_a v_i) w_i term using L6-L2-L3 Holder exponents. convFormH1 is the sum of those integrals with the derivative on its second argument. The public IBP statement separates the divergence term and transfers the derivative to the third argument. The divergence theorem gives zero trace-gradient pairing against every real Schwartz scalar test. The final statement proves antisymmetry in the second/third slots under its stated H1/solenoidal hypotheses.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  All fields lie in the upstream L2VF_R3 carrier with proofs of memH1VF_R3. The integrability theorem requires no solenoidality. The public IBP signature explicitly requires u and w in L2Sigma_R3, even where proof parameter names start with an underscore. The public antisymmetry signature explicitly requires all three fields solenoidal; retain even the unused-looking v premise. The divergence statement takes an arbitrary real Schwartz test and a solenoidal H1 field.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Identify upstream component projections and gradComp_of_memH1 with the native real-vector L2 fields and compact-test weak gradients. Port the weak-Leibniz, density and real/complex Lp dependencies, or reproduce the proof on native PhysicalH1. These spatial statements then support the L2 energy/error cancellation in I06/I11. The private divFree_L2test helper is an internal proof pattern, not a public name to import. For the H1 error pairing, separately prove all products and integrations by parts involving the actual Laplacian of the error and the projected reference.

LIMITATIONS
  Spatial cancellation does not construct the projected NSE trajectory or show time differentiability/absolute continuity of its energies. It supplies no pressure cancellation against the Laplacian, no quantitative H1 product estimate, and no I10 residual or I11 full inhomogeneous H1 differential inequality. The H1/divergence-free assumptions are literal type premises; no stronger cancellation statement is inferred by deleting underscore-named arguments.

DECLARATIONS

            (L2VF_projComponentC_R3 a u x).re *

EX05 — Accepted physical H2 graph supplies the spectral derivative witnesses
==============================================================================

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Constructs SchwartzDerivativeWitnesses for the complexified velocity, all three actual L2 gradients and the actual L2 Laplacian of a native PhysicalH2 value. It supplies EX02's derivative-witness premise from the physical graph.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  An actual u : PhysicalH2. The weak gradient and Hessian identities are fields of that native graph; no new spectral-realization premise is assumed.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  For the interpolation construction, compose EX05 with EX02 nativeSpectralRealization_of_schwartzDerivativeWitnesses and EX01 interpolation_of_nativeSpectralRealization, then rewrite complexified L2 norms with the existing real-to-complex isometry. This gives the precise lower-source chain for the intended physical squared-energy interpolation wrapper. Cutoff/H2 admission and the wrapper's matching external evidence remain separate endpoints.

LIMITATIONS
  This theorem consumes an existing PhysicalH2 value. It does not produce such a value from a literal global NSE-A solution or arbitrary weighted Fourier data; it also does not supply H1-to-L6, H2-to-Linfinity, convection, or time-evolution estimates.

==============================================================================
UC01 — Common contraction modulus gives equicontinuity
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Topology/MetricSpace/Equicontinuity.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Topology.MetricSpace.Equicontinuity
Git blob: 372b881acc63bea86f4cb9c902fcc7b854fb76aa
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/MetricSpace/Equicontinuity.lean
Namespace: Metric
Plan steps: I02, I10
Reuse classification: PINNED_GENERIC_MATHLIB_SUPPORT
Evidence status:
  PINNED SOURCE inspected at the Material Mathlib dependency revision. No separate retained named audit is attached to this declaration and no new compiler or kernel verification was run.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Turns one common metric continuity modulus into equicontinuity of the entire indexed family.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Ambient variables: {alpha beta iota : Type*}, [PseudoMetricSpace alpha], [PseudoMetricSpace beta]. Supply b : Real -> Real tending to 0 at 0, F : iota -> beta -> alpha, and the common distance bound for every index and every pair.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  After I02 constructs actual J_n : PhysicalH1 ->L[Real] PhysicalH1 and proves ||J_n|| <= 1, restrict J_n to the compact image K = U([0,T]) in PhysicalH1. Use b(r)=r and F(n,x)=J_n(x). The inherited metric on K retains the same contraction estimate. This supplies the equicontinuity input of UC02.

LIMITATIONS
  The premise is a uniform operator estimate, not continuity of each J_n separately. This declaration does not construct a multiplier, supply pointwise cutoff convergence, prove compactness of K, or provide a rate.

DECLARATIONS

  UC01.01 — Metric.equicontinuous_of_continuity_modulus
  Kind: theorem
  Source statement lines: 112–114
  Source signature:
    theorem equicontinuous_of_continuity_modulus {ι : Type*} [PseudoMetricSpace β] (b : ℝ → ℝ)
        (b_lim : Tendsto b (𝓝 0) (𝓝 0)) (F : ι → β → α)
        (H : ∀ (x y : β) (i), dist (F i x) (F i y) ≤ b (dist x y)) : Equicontinuous F
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

EVIDENCE / SOURCE POINTERS
  https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/MetricSpace/Equicontinuity.lean
    Exact dependency-pinned source inspected statically. Git blob identity and source statement line span are retained.

==============================================================================
UC02 — Pointwise-to-uniform convergence on a compact reference image
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/Ascoli.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Topology.UniformSpace.Ascoli
Git blob: 5c7c248f62fcc5657b6b152b9955d128adbbc898
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/Ascoli.lean
Namespace: Equicontinuous
Plan steps: I02, I10
Reuse classification: PINNED_GENERIC_MATHLIB_SUPPORT
Evidence status:
  PINNED SOURCE inspected at the Material Mathlib dependency revision. No separate retained named audit is attached to this declaration and no new compiler or kernel verification was run.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  For an equicontinuous family on a compact domain, convergence in the uniform-function topology is equivalent to pointwise/product-topology convergence.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Ambient {iota X alpha : Type*}, [TopologicalSpace X], [UniformSpace alpha], {F : iota -> X -> alpha}; [CompactSpace X]. Supply Equicontinuous F, a filter, and the limit function. The right side is convergence in the Pi/product topology, assembled from pointwise convergence.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Take X=K, the compact subtype image of the continuous reference U on [0,T], alpha=PhysicalH1, iota=Nat, filter=atTop, F(n,x)=J_n(x), and f(x)=x. UC01 provides equicontinuity; PC12 with MV12 and the new cutoff formula provides the pointwise H1 convergence. Convert the conclusion with UC03, then pull it back along U to obtain uniform-in-time H1 error. The quantitative H2 tail alternative in the v0.2 construction note can avoid this compactness adapter for the plan's supplied H2 reference.

LIMITATIONS
  Compactness is required only of the reference image, not of all H1 norm balls or the bandlimited carrier. This is a generic compact-uniform convergence theorem, not a finite-dimensional approximation theorem and not a nonlinear convergence result.

DECLARATIONS

  UC02.01 — Equicontinuous.tendsto_uniformFun_iff_pi
  Kind: theorem
  Source statement lines: 166–169
  Source signature:
    theorem Equicontinuous.tendsto_uniformFun_iff_pi [CompactSpace X]
        (F_eqcont : Equicontinuous F) (ℱ : Filter ι) (f : X → α) :
        Tendsto (UniformFun.ofFun ∘ F) ℱ (𝓝 <| UniformFun.ofFun f) ↔
        Tendsto F ℱ (𝓝 f)
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

EVIDENCE / SOURCE POINTERS
  https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/Ascoli.lean
    Exact dependency-pinned source inspected statically. Git blob identity and source statement line span are retained.

==============================================================================
UC03 — Convert uniform-function topology to explicit uniform convergence
==============================================================================
Repository: leanprover-community/mathlib4
Revision: db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d
Source path: Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/UniformConvergenceTopology.lean
Import module: Mathlib.Topology.UniformSpace.UniformConvergenceTopology
Git blob: 0d4026c0018bbc9489e4f9e4e3b7e141a070fe15
Source URL: https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/UniformConvergenceTopology.lean
Namespace: UniformFun
Plan steps: I02, I10
Reuse classification: PINNED_GENERIC_MATHLIB_SUPPORT
Evidence status:
  PINNED SOURCE inspected at the Material Mathlib dependency revision. No separate retained named audit is attached to this declaration and no new compiler or kernel verification was run.

WHAT IT SUPPLIES
  Identifies convergence in the UniformFun topology with TendstoUniformly of the underlying unbundled functions.

REQUIRED INPUTS / AMBIENT CONTEXT
  Ambient arbitrary index/domain types, a uniform-space codomain, filter p, F : iota -> (alpha -> uniform beta), and f in that uniform-function space. Preserve the UniformConvergence notation and UniformFun.ofFun/toFun coercions from the source.

ADAPTER STILL NEEDED
  Use this equivalence after UC02 to obtain TendstoUniformly (fun n x => J_n x) (fun x => x) atTop on compact subtype K. In the metric codomain this yields the epsilon/eventually/forall-x estimate. The delta_n supremum requires boundedness of the actual continuous error on the nonempty compact time interval, or can be replaced by explicit uniform epsilon estimates until a scalar sequence is packaged.

LIMITATIONS
  This declaration only rewrites a convergence notion. It supplies no equicontinuity, compactness, pointwise convergence, boundedness, norm estimate, or cutoff construction.

DECLARATIONS

  UC03.01 — UniformFun.tendsto_iff_tendstoUniformly
  Kind: theorem
  Source statement lines: 455–456
  Source signature:
    protected theorem tendsto_iff_tendstoUniformly {F : ι → α →ᵤ β} {f : α →ᵤ β} :
        Tendsto F p (𝓝 f) ↔ TendstoUniformly (toFun ∘ F) (toFun f) p
  No separate named acceptance record is attached to this declaration
  in this index; use the precise card-level source/evidence classification.

EVIDENCE / SOURCE POINTERS
  https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d/Mathlib/Topology/UniformSpace/UniformConvergenceTopology.lean
    Exact dependency-pinned source inspected statically. Git blob identity and source statement line span are retained.

6. SCOPE GUARDS AND REMAINING SOURCE WORK
  - LC03: SmoothSigmaZero and GlobalSmoothNSE are periodic predicates despite their Euclidean coordinate type. They cannot serve as the literal R3 target.
  - MV10: uniform_local_time, solution_agree, and forwardGlobalSolution_exists retain FiniteDimensional at this pin. The energy lemmas in MV09 omit it; the private tiling helper in MV11 omits it only after a uniform local-time premise is supplied.
  - MV11: solve_exists_on_step is private. Its source name is a search location, not a public importable constant.
  - PC05: the reverse Fourier derivative equivalence receives actual L2 derivative witnesses. Constructing those witnesses from a bandlimited field is still work.
  - PC13: a six-sector projection theorem is a proof pattern for the ball mask. It does not instantiate a ball projection under a renamed identifier.
  - SC01: epsilon_n must eventually be strictly positive; use the derived residual bound plus 1/(n+1) when needed. Interior derivatives and closed-slab continuity are explicit hypotheses.
  - SC03 and SC07: scalar reciprocals use the original normalization; physical gradient enstrophy is not half-enstrophy. Preserve the e_n=(1/2)||grad u_n||^2 adapter.
  - SC05–SC06: the accepted periodic family theorems are patterns only. Their scalar sublemmas can be reused, but their physical family cannot be retyped as R3.
  - SC07–SC10: the inspected accepted cross-branch files are beyond main's frozen integrated AC baseline. A later import/integration must preserve exact sources and obtain its own matching evidence.
  - SC08: positive-time H2 heat trajectories and their zero extensions for integration must not replace the original t=0 L2/H1 initial trace.
  - SC09–SC10: continuous L2 Duhamel response from time-L2/spatial-L2 forcing does not prove the forced derivative equation, H1/H2 maximal regularity, or nonlinear NSE evolution.
  - The H2Interpolation and accompanying newer H2 heat batch at the inspected AC21b4ae85 snapshot remains prepared pending matching external evidence. It is not counted as an accepted ingredient here.
  - The compact-C1 Sobolev anchor in MV04 still needs physical H1 density/Kato/tensor adapters. This index does not claim a completed native H1-to-L6 or H2-to-Linfinity package.
  - For the generic H1 approximation route, weighted Fourier tail convergence uses MV12 after PC12/PC11 identifies the actual integrable density; UC gives the compact-image uniformity adapter. With a uniform H2 bound, section 4.1 supplies a quantitative alternative target.
  - AN01–AN02: actual Lp multiplication retains the Holder exponent relation and both input memberships. It does not supply the missing physical Sobolev or Bernstein bounds.
  - AN04–AN05: inner and squared-norm calculus require actual time derivatives. Spatial/time derivative commutation and convection cancellation remain separate physical arguments.
  - EP: the accepted periodic cross-transport assembly uses coefficientEnergy_le_dissipation. Its spectral-gap bound is unavailable on R3; retain the inhomogeneous H1 error and replace the product estimates.
  - EP: align the exact reference-residual sign before importing the error algebra. Periodic C1 supremum bounds do not follow from the plan's C([0,T];H2) reference; use the L6 x L3 and Linfinity x L2 estimates instead.
  - EX: EX05 supplies actual distributional derivative witnesses from PhysicalH2, and EX02 discharges EX01's spectral-realization premise. The composed physical-norm wrapper remains new. External H1 convection cutouts retain their original types and toolchain, with no native acceptance inferred.
  - UC: compactness concerns the image of the reference trajectory, not an infinite-dimensional H1 ball. The quantitative H2 tail note in section 4.1 is a new adapter target, not an already accepted theorem.
  - No indexed theorem constructs the sharp-cutoff nonlinear family, proves its actual H1 error bound, proves literal closed-slab admission, or closes the R3 Ehrenfest root. These remain new plan endpoints.
  - StoppedCommutatorBoundR3 witness existence remains open and is not supplied by source indexing.
